# Open-Source 3D LUT Mega-Vault (iPhone 17 Blackmagic Camera & Cinema Edition)

**Total Unique, Verified & Auto-Labeled `.cube` LUTs:** `1608` | **Duplicates Purged:** `733`

## Folder Architecture
- `luts/1_Apple_Log_2_iPhone17Pro/` — Engineered strictly for iPhone 17 Pro / Pro Max (Apple Log 2 Wide Gamut)
- `luts/2_Apple_Log_Original/` — Engineered for standard iPhone Pro Apple Log workflows
- `luts/3_Rec709_Creative_Looks/` — Creative & Stylized grades for Base iPhone 17 / Rec. 709 footage
- `luts/4_Camera_Log_and_Film_Print/` — Kodak 2383, Fujifilm 3513, ARRI LogC, Sony S-Log2/3, V-Log & DJI D-Log LUTs
- `luts/5_Desktop_Mastering_65pt/` — High-precision 65-point desktop LUTs for DaVinci Resolve / Premiere / FCP
